# Zone server: packet field layouts (generated)

Generated by `tools/protocol-re/layouts.py` + `packets.py` from Ghidra decompilation of the shipped binary.
Offsets are **payload** offsets (byte 0 = first byte after the 2-byte opcode). Little-endian.

How to read the *Value / source* column:
- `` `123` `` constant written by the server.
- `this[0x…]`: member of the sending object (session or player) at that byte offset.
- `in(0xOPCODE)+N`: copied from byte N of the incoming packet whose handler sent this one (shows data flow).
- Other text: the decompiler expression, shortened.
- Gaps between offsets are bytes the code did not write (padding, or filled in a way the extractor missed).
- **Fill**: part of the payload is produced by a helper; for zone, virtual helpers are resolved assuming a `ShinePlayer` receiver.
- Names: official enum names from the 2016 server PDBs (work item S01) where the value is in them.
- †: name neither from the 2016 server PDBs nor from the opcode's own 2016 handler (2017 client PDB, REQ+1 = ACK guess, or a log string).

- **2016 PDB layout**: the `PROTO_*` struct from the 2016 server PDBs (`docs/protocol/pdb2016-layouts.json`, work item S01,
  same build), shown when no hand-verified layout exists. Otherwise **2017 PDB layout**: the struct from the 2017 client
  PDB (`docs/protocol/pdb-layouts.json`, work item C02), a newer build: confirm against the 2016 code before relying on it.

Heuristic extraction: verify a layout against the cited function before relying on it.

## Shared structures (hand-verified)

### AVATAR (130 bytes)

Basis: Character server FUN_0046bff0 (reads SQL columns in this order with SQLGetData) and WM FUN_0040ede0; field names = PROTO_AVATARINFORMATION (pdb2017), every offset agrees (C02 §5)

| Off | Type | Field | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | u32 | chrregnum | character id; L00 name 'char_no' |
| 4 | char[20] | name | L00 name 'char_name' |
| 24 | i16 | level | SQL_C_SSHORT column; L00 name 'level (inferred)' |
| 26 | u8 | slot | 0..5 |
| 27 | char[12] | loginmap | WM substitutes "RouT" when another field is 0; L00 name 'login_map (inferred)' |
| 39 | u8[5] | delinfo | if a 'pending delete' column is set: year+'0', month, day, hour, minute; else byte 39 = 0; {year, month, day, hour, min} u8 each; L00 name 'delete_time (inferred)' |
| 44 | u8 | shape.race|chrclass|gender | bits 0-1, bits 2-6, bit 7 (three separate u8 columns packed); bits race:2@0, chrclass:5@2, gender:1@7; L00 name 'race:2 | class:5 | gender:1 (inferred)' |
| 45 | u8 | shape.hairtype | L00 name 'hair (inferred)' |
| 46 | u8 | shape.haircolor | L00 name 'hair_color (inferred)' |
| 47 | u8 | shape.faceshape | L00 name 'face (inferred)' |
| 48 | u16[20] | equip | 0xFFFF = empty. Equip-slot -> offset: 1:48, 24:50, 12:52, 7:54, 10:56, 19:58, 21:60, 22:62, 20:64, 8:66, 27:68, 28:70, 26:72, 11:74, 13:76, 9:78, 17:80, 18:82, 25:84, 29:86; PDB order: Equ_Head, Mouth, RightHand, Body, LeftHand, Pant, Boot, AccBoot, AccPant, AccBody, AccHeadA, MiniMon_R, Eye, AccLeftHand, AccRightHand, AccBack, CosEff, AccHip, Minimon, AccShield; L00 name 'equipment item ids' |
| 88 | u8[3] | equip.upgrade | 4-bit nibbles: 88 lo=slot10 hi=slot12; 89 lo=slot7 hi=slot19; 90 lo=slot21; bits lefthand:4, righthand:4 | body:4, leg:4 | shoes:4; L00 name 'upgrade levels' |
| 91 | u32 | nKQHandle | SQL_C_SLONG column; L00 name '?' |
| 95 | char[12] | sKQMapName | L00 name '? (second map name)' |
| 107 | u32 | nKQCoord.x | SQL_C_ULONG column; L00 name '?' |
| 111 | u32 | nKQCoord.y | SQL_C_ULONG column; L00 name '?' |
| 115 | u32 | dKQDate | bits: year&15 | month<<4 | day<<8 | hour<<13 | minute<<18 | second<<24; top 2 bits kept; bits year:4@0, month:4@4, day:5@8, hour:5@13, min:6@18, sec:6@24; L00 name 'packed timestamp' |
| 119 | u8[2] | CharIDChangeData.bNeedChangeID|bInit | 0; two u8: bNeedChangeID @119 (0xB409 requires 1), bInit @120; L00 name '?' |
| 121 | u32 | CharIDChangeData.nRowNo | 0; L00 name '?' |
| 125 | u32 | TutorialInfo.nTutorialState | default 3 when no tutorial row; L00 name 'tutorial_state (inferred)' |
| 129 | u8 | TutorialInfo.nTutorialStep | L00 name 'tutorial_step (inferred)' |

### PROTO_SELL_ITEM_INFO_SERVER (122 bytes)

Basis: 2016 Zone.pdb PROTO_SELL_ITEM_INFO_SERVER (size 0x7a, a maximum): first byte nDataSize = bytes that follow (P02 batch 2: capture:shop 0x4037 / 0x1130 elements of 22 bytes with nDataSize 21); named `datasize` so that pcap_decode.py reads it as a variable-length element

| Off | Type | Field | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | u8 | datasize | nDataSize (pdb2016): length of the rest of the element |
| 1 | u16 | nHandle |  |
| 3 | u64 | nSellItemKey | SHINE_ITEM_REGISTNUMBER; −1 in capture:shop |
| 11 | bytes | ItemTotalInfo | ItemTotalInformation, nDataSize − 10 bytes (item id first: 3000, 3001 in capture:shop) |

### ITEM_INVEN (2 bytes)

Basis: pdb2017 union ITEM_INVEN { Pos:10, Type:6 } (2 bytes); Z04 §1.1. P02 batch 2 part 11: no file defined it, so the decoder read every ITEM_INVEN field (D03 / D05 / Z04 annotations) as 1 byte

| Off | Type | Field | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | u16 | inven | type << 10 | slot (pdb2017 union Pos:10 / Type:6; Z04 §1.1). P02 part 11: without this entry the decoder read every ITEM_INVEN as 1 byte |

one shared buffer per drop: the pickable bit is rewritten for each receiver just before the send; re-broadcast to the sight when the owner-only time ends and when a dice winner fails to pick

### `0x201F` NC_ACT_SOMEONESHOUT_CMD (hand-named) (S→C)

**Verified layout** (basis: zone ShineMob::so_Shout FUN_00459250, ShineMobileObject::so_Shout FUN_00459080; client handler reads offset 1 as u16 for mobs (re-data/client-handlers.json 0x201f); 2017 PDB PROTO_NC_ACT_SOMEONESHOUT_CMD agrees; Lua cSomebodyShout FUN_005e5150; Pine someoneshout ShineSomeoneShout::sa_Step FUN_004eeeb0 (Z09 §26.2))

| Off | Type | Field | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | u8 | itemLinkDataCount | 0 for mobs; link count for players; never written by Lua cSomebodyShout (stale, Z09-B71) |
| 1 | char[20] | speaker | union: charID (player name, or a script-given name) / mobID (vt 0x708) when isMob |
| 21 | u8 | flag | bits GMColor:1@0 (vt 0xef4), isMob:1@1 (pdb2016; mob version: speaker string empty); other bits stale in the mob version |
| 22 | u8 | len | content length |
| 23 | char[len] | content | text, then itemLinkDataCount × 0x67-byte item links (player version only) |

to the whole map instance (target 0xFFFF) or one object. The mob version sends len + 0x1A bytes including the opcode, one stale byte more than the packet (Z09-B10). The client shows nothing for len 0 or > 128. Pine someoneshout writes speaker = 20 bytes of a dialog string, clears GMColor and isMob, never writes itemLinkDataCount (Z09-B112) and sends len + 0x19.

### `0x245B` NC_BAT_REFLECTIONDAMAGE_CMD (hand-named) (S→C)

**Verified layout** (basis: zone FUN_0055dbc0 (ShineMobileObject::smo_ReflectionDamage), broadcast to the attacker's sight; client GameFrameWork FUN_004b6980)

| Off | Type | Field | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | u16 | abstate | ABSTATEINDEX of the reflector's first state with SAA_REFLECTDAMAGE; client: picks the effect played on the reflector; 14 / 339 / 0x318 suppress the damage number |
| 2 | u16 | ReflectionObject | handle of the reflecting defender |
| 4 | u16 | TargetObject | handle of the attacker who takes the reflected damage |
| 6 | u32 | resthp | attacker HP after the damage; client: 0 = death animation |

| 19 | u16 | attr | 0 when deleting, else 0x15 = (attribute start − buffer) − 2, the payload offset of the attribute block |
| 21 | bytes | attribute | only when isdelete = 0: the item's attribute block with lot − 1 (item class vt 4) |

payload 0x15 bytes (0x17 with the opcode) when deleting, else + attribute block at payload offset attr = 0x15 (computed in the code); to the GameDB socket. Answers 0x9007-0x900A (Z08 §11); DB side D03. Z23 §4.
